# HANDOFF — Session Continuity Document

> **Purpose of this file:** This is the living memory of the PaperTrader project. If you are a new Claude session (Cowork or Claude Code) picking this up cold, read this file FIRST, then `README.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`, and `ROADMAP.md`. It records who we are, what we've decided, what's done, what's next, and every important decision and its reasoning — so no context is ever lost between sessions.
>
> **Maintenance rule:** Claude (acting as CTO in Cowork) updates this file at the end of any meaningful exchange — new decisions, completed work, changed direction, new blockers. Append to the Changelog at the bottom every time. Keep it elaborate. When in doubt, over-document.

**Last updated:** 2026-08-14 (`verify-hardening-pass.ts` flake FIXED at the root, not tolerated blind — it had failed twice on a ~$2 live-price mismatch and passed clean on isolated re-run both times, exactly the "intermittently-red teaches you to ignore failures" pattern that lets a real regression slip through later. Root cause: the test's own "Dashboard total" reconstruction fetched each holding's price through `getServerQuote`'s 30-second-TTL cache, then compared it against `getPositionsValue()`'s (and `runSnapshots()`'s) internal fetch, which is DELIBERATELY always-live/never-cached for a money-critical number (`valuation.server.ts`'s own doc comment: "Never a cached/stale number for a money decision"). Comparing a value up to 30s stale against an always-fresh one is real, unavoidable drift on an active trading day, not a bug in either code path. **Fixed by eliminating the avoidable half of the gap** (candidate (a), preferred per instruction): switched the test's own holdings-value calc from a per-symbol loop through the cache to ONE batched, uncached `providerQuotes()` call — the exact same mechanism `getPositionsValue()` uses internally — shrinking the residual race window from "up to 30 real seconds" to "the network gap between two back-to-back live fetches." Added a small, explicitly-justified tolerance (`closeToLive`, 5bps of the reference value or 2¢, whichever is larger) for the 3 assertions that legitimately still span two independent live fetches to two separate real production functions (not mockable without defeating the point of a zero-mocks integration suite) — calibrated to be ~1000× tighter than the smallest real bug this test exists to catch (the original missing-loan-subtraction bug it's named for). Verified properly, not with one lucky run: ran the full 30-script suite **three consecutive times — 30/30 green on all three**, zero `❌` markers anywhere, and the specific reconciliation numbers matched EXACTLY to the cent in all three runs (the tolerance never had to do any work — it's a documented safety margin, not a crutch papering over a real mismatch). `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` clean. Committed locally — NOT pushed, reporting first per standing convention. Full detail in the changelog entry below.)

**🚀 STRATEGIC SHIFT (2026-08-04): this is now a REAL PRODUCT for strangers, web first, mobile later. See `PLAN.md` (new, at repo root) — it is the strategic layer above this file; HANDOFF stays the execution memory.** Key decisions locked: multi-tenancy ALREADY EXISTS (RLS — don't rebuild it) · "increase risk for successful users" REJECTED as harmful, replaced with adaptive *coaching* + progressive unlocks · issue tracking = **GitHub Projects** (not Jira: no connector, but `gh` CLI is installed+authed so Claude Code can file/close issues and link them to commits) · **spend engineering before money** — the durable Postgres price cache is free and worth ~10–50× more concurrent users than the current in-memory cache (which dies every serverless invocation). Phases: **A** account management/abuse guards/ops/legal (P0, blocks open signup) → **B** moat (trade journal, behavioural analytics, adaptive coaching, super-admin) → **C** payments → **D** scale → **E** mobile. Confirmed by Venky: monetization LATER, classroom mode NOT NOW, and the free/paid tier split is agreed (see PLAN.md §C).
**💰 MONETIZATION DECIDED (2026-08-04, see PLAN.md §C for full reasoning):** starting capital **$100,000 → $25,000** on EDUCATIONAL grounds (forces position-sizing discipline; $10k rejected as too low for options/margin to remain usable). **Selling virtual cash REJECTED** — Venky proposed it, CTO pushed back (destroys the risk-management lesson, competes with the free Reset button, low willingness-to-pay, becomes a 30%-cut in-app purchase on mobile), Venky agreed. **Paid instead:** multiple portfolios · configurable starting capital (a setting, not a purchase) · **scenario challenges** ("Trade the 2008 crash" — Venky's favourite, strong differentiator, runs on existing historical-candle infra). Free forever: paper trading, portfolio, watchlists, basic charts, journal, and the public what-if simulator (the viral hook — never paywall). **Build entitlement checks + usage metering into Phase B rather than retrofitting in Phase C.**
**✅ PLAN.md build-order STEP 9 (B5 — scenario challenges) COMPLETE — PUSHED to origin/main (`9fb73d9`).** Migration `0025_scenario_challenges.sql` applied by Venky. "Trade the 2008 crash" — 3 curated scenarios (2008 financial crisis, 2020 COVID crash, 2022 bear market), a fixed $10,000 stake per attempt, week-stepping (5 trading days per "Advance" click, justified since day-stepping would be 150–250 clicks), fully isolated from the real paper account (own tables/cash/holdings, same isolation pattern as the AI agent's sub-portfolio). No-look-ahead — the property this feature lives or dies on — is enforced structurally (the DB's own `step_index` is the only progress source, one server function is the only candle-data code path, and it slices to that cutoff) and was proven at the network-payload level in the browser, not just asserted in scripts. Full detail, including two mid-build incidents that were fixed structurally rather than patched, in the changelog entry below.

**📋 BUILD ORDER LOCKED (CTO-sequenced, Venky delegated — see PLAN.md §6 for the full table):** 1 Account mgmt + $25k · 2 **Durable price cache (pulled FORWARD from Phase D — free, ~10–50× capacity, must precede growth)** · 3 Live prices + live intraday chart (the visible payoff of #2; nearly free on top of it, prohibitive without) · 4 Options discoverability (Venky couldn't find options at all — they're only a Stock Detail tab) · 5 Abuse guards + ops + legal (ALL required before open public signup) · 6 Trade journal · 7 Behavioural analytics · 8 Adaptive coaching + progressive unlocks · 9 Scenario challenges · 10 Super-admin · 11 Payments (+ triggers Vercel Pro licensing + legal review) · 12 Paid data tier · 13 Mobile.

- **✅ FOLLOW-UP CHUNK 1 (repair `verify-insights.ts`) COMPLETE — committed locally.** NOT pushed. Brought up to the hardened-harness standard (whole-body try/catch + explicit `process.exit`, every await wrapped in a per-step timeout, timestamped before/after logging). Root-caused the original hang class (no timeouts anywhere in the old script — any stalled Claude/Finnhub/Supabase call would block forever with vite-node's non-exit-on-uncaught-exception quirk); the hardened rewrite completes in ~15–20s with zero hangs across two real runs. Along the way found the 3 initial "failing" assertions were NOT a product bug: `service_role` genuinely has no `DELETE` grant on `insights` (an already-documented gap from the V1 Insights work), so the test's own state-reset step silently no-ops and a same-day cached row makes "exactly 1 Claude call" a false assumption — fixed by checking ground truth (does a same-day row already exist?) before asserting the expected call count, rather than assuming a reset that structurally can't happen. Full detail in the changelog entry below.
- **✅ FOLLOW-UP CHUNK 2 (disclose borrowed-vs-cash split in trade dialogs) COMPLETE — committed locally.** NOT pushed. New pure `lib/margin/borrowSplit.ts` (`computeBorrowSplit`/`borrowSplitSentence`) computes the trivial cash-first/borrow-remainder split from `cashBalance`/`marginLoan` already fetched via `getMarginState` — never a re-derivation of buying power/equity/maintenance. Wired into both the stock buy `ConfirmDialog` (Shares + Dollars modes, `app.stock.$symbol.tsx`) and the option buy-to-open `ConfirmDialog` (`OptionOrderPanel.tsx`, sell-to-close untouched — it never borrows): when margin is enabled AND the order would draw on the loan, the dialog appends "This would draw about $X from cash and borrow about $Y on margin — your loan would go from $A to about $B, and interest accrues daily at N%" plus a From cash / Borrowed on margin / Margin loan before→after breakdown in the detail box; margin off or a fully cash-covered order shows NEITHER, reading byte-identical to the pre-margin dialog. Live-verified all four required states with DB read-backs matching the dialog's prediction to the cent in every case (margin off: dialog unchanged; margin on + fully covered: no borrow text; margin on + partial cover, stock Dollars mode: $95,000 from cash / $25,000 borrowed → DB loan $25,000.00 exactly; margin on + partial cover, stock Shares mode; option buy-to-open: loan $25,000→$27,416.00 predicted, DB confirmed $27,416.00 exactly). Desktop screenshots clean; a true 375px pixel screenshot hit a session-specific Browser-pane scaling artifact (viewport reported 481px instead of 375px on every tab, confirmed via a fresh tab too) — verified via `getBoundingClientRect` instead that the detail box (421px) sits comfortably inside the actual rendered dialog width (481px) with 30px to spare on each side, i.e. no real CSS overflow, a tool limitation not a product bug. Full detail in the changelog entry below.

**📐 CTO LESSON (2026-08-09) — THE GRANT/SILENT-WRITE PATTERN HAS NOW BITTEN US 3×. Make it structural.** Instances: `transactions` missing service_role SELECT (found in M1) · `insights` missing DELETE (found repairing verify-insights) · `analytics_events` missing DELETE (found because the CTO asked whether the privacy policy's retention promise was actually honoured — the prune had been silently failing since it shipped). Same shape every time: a new table is created, the code performs an operation nobody granted, and it fails SILENTLY because the return value isn't checked. **STANDING RULE for every new table from now on: (1) enumerate EVERY operation the code will perform on it (select/insert/update/delete, by which role) and grant exactly those in the same migration; (2) route the write through `mustSucceed()`/`logIfFailed()` so a permission failure can never be silent; (3) if the table has a retention/prune path, the DELETE grant is part of the feature, not an afterthought.** Corollary: any user-facing PROMISE (privacy retention, "we delete your data") must have a test that proves it, or it's aspirational.

**📐 CTO LESSON (2026-08-04, from the epic hardening pass): specify DERIVED numbers, not just the obvious ones.** The equity bug (dashboard/portfolio-%/snapshots showed gross assets, not equity, for margin users — overstating net worth by the exact loan amount) existed because the M1/M2 specs said "make BUYING POWER margin-aware" and never said "net the loan out of TOTAL PORTFOLIO VALUE." CC built exactly what was asked. **Rule going forward: when a new liability/asset class is introduced, enumerate EVERY derived figure it touches (totals, %s, charts, snapshots, exports) in the spec — don't assume the obvious surface is the only one.** The hardening phase caught it, which is why cross-feature seam audits are non-negotiable at epic close.

**🚨 VERIFICATION-HARNESS RULE (learned the hard way 5×: 10.4 snapshots, Insights brief, M1 margin, 2026-08-11's scenario-challenges debug script (~2h silent hang), and 2026-08-12's admin-console browser-verification seed script (~4h24m silent hang, killed by CTO order — see the dedicated bullet below).** ROOT CAUSE finally identified during M1: an uncaught top-level `throw` in a `vite-node` script does NOT exit the process (unlike plain Node) — the SSR module runtime stays resident forever, printing nothing. Every "hang" was likely this, not slow APIs and not stuck DB locks. **MANDATORY for every verification script from now on:** (1) wrap the whole body in try/catch with an explicit `process.exit(1)`; (2) wrap EVERY await in a hard per-step timeout that throws with the step name; (3) print a timestamped line BEFORE and AFTER every step; (4) run in the foreground under a pty. Also: if a verification run goes quiet for >10 min, KILL IT — do not wait on a notification. **This is now STRUCTURAL, not just a convention to remember:** `app/verify-harness.ts` exports `step`/`assert`/`approx`/`deepEqual`/`sleep`/`createTestUser`/`runVerification` — every verify-*.ts script (including ad hoc throwaway debug scripts written under time pressure) MUST import from it rather than hand-rolling these primitives. `runVerification(main, opts)` wraps the ENTIRE script body in one hard global-ceiling timeout (default 8 min) as a backstop independent of any individual step's own timeout, so even a script that forgets to wrap one particular await still can't hang past the ceiling.
- **A second, compounding incident on the SAME 2026-08-11 debug script, root-caused separately:** the 2-hour hang's real trigger was `admin.auth.admin.createUser(...)` called WITHOUT `user_metadata: { terms_accepted_version: ... }` — `handle_new_user()` (migration `0022_terms_acceptance.sql`) REQUIRES that field and `RAISE EXCEPTION`s mid-transaction if it's absent (by design — a real signup-form bug should fail loudly, not silently create an unconsented account). For an `admin.auth.admin.createUser` CALLER, that trigger exception surfaces as an OPAQUE Postgres/GoTrue `500` (`AuthRetryableFetchError`, empty message) — nothing about it says "you forgot the metadata," and it was first misdiagnosed as a Supabase platform outage before the Auth error logs (`Failed to close prepared statement: ERROR: current transaction is aborted...`) made the real cause legible. Audited EVERY `createUser` call site in the repo (not just the ones from memory): all ~20 pre-existing verify-*.ts call sites already had the field correctly — the gap was entirely in throwaway scratch scripts that didn't copy that pattern. **Fixed structurally, not just for this one incident:** `createTestUser(admin, email, password)` in `verify-harness.ts` is now the ONLY way any script creates a test user — it always attaches the required metadata, so forgetting it is no longer possible. Every existing verify-*.ts call site was retrofitted to use it (mechanical swap, no behavior change, confirmed via typecheck).
- **Production implication worth knowing (not a bug to fix, just a sharp edge):** because `handle_new_user()` raises on missing metadata, a REAL caller who somehow hit this path (impossible via our own signup form, which always sends `terms_accepted_version` — see A4/`0022`) would see an opaque `500`/`AuthRetryableFetchError`, not our friendly "terms not accepted" message. The clear error only exists at the Postgres/trigger level, invisible to the HTTP caller. Low risk (our own client can't trigger it), but worth remembering if a future integration ever calls `auth.admin.createUser`/`auth.signUp` without going through the app's own signup form.
- **Supabase project limits worth remembering for future test-user seeding:** this project's Supabase instance caps outbound auth emails at **2/hour** (the cause of earlier email-send failures during testing — mitigated by always passing `email_confirm: true` in test scripts, which skips the confirmation email entirely) and caps signups at **30 per 5 minutes**. Pace any script that creates more than a handful of throwaway users in a tight loop.
- **🚨 2026-08-12 — a THIRD hang, ~4h24m silent, killed by explicit CTO order.** A one-off seed script for admin-console browser verification (creating a throwaway user + a journal entry, run via `runVerification` with a 30s global ceiling) never printed even its first `console.log` and never exited despite that ceiling — ps showed 4h24m wall time against only ~21min CPU time (blocked on I/O, not spinning). **ROOT CAUSE, confirmed by direct inspection: `lib/supabase/admin.server.ts`'s `getServiceClient()` had ZERO timeout on ANY call** — auth admin API, postgrest, rpc, all of it, via the plain `createClient(...)` with no `global.fetch` override. This is the EXACT gap the 2026-08-11 incident already identified and explicitly deferred ("a residual risk, deliberately not touched … to avoid regressions across every feature using `getServiceClient()`") — that deferral is what let this recur. **Fixed at the source, not just for this one script:** `getServiceClient()` now passes a custom `global.fetch` (20s `AbortController` timeout) into `createClient()` — since supabase-js routes `auth.*`/`.from()`/`.rpc()` ALL through this one function (confirmed by reading `SupabaseClient.ts`'s `_initSupabaseAuthClient` call), this is a single unavoidable choke point that protects every current and future caller, not a per-call-site fix. `createTestUser()` in `verify-harness.ts` also got its OWN redundant 25s `withTimeout` wrap (belt-and-suspenders — it can't hang past 25s even if some future `admin` client it's handed lacks a timeout of its own). **The browser-side client (`lib/supabase/client.ts`) had the identical gap** (zero fetch timeout, meaning a stalled connection could spin a real signed-in user's tab forever) — fixed the same way, 30s (vs. 20s server-side, since real user network conditions vary more).
  - **NEW STANDING RULE, no exceptions: there are no "quick throwaway scripts."** Both multi-hour losses (2026-08-11 and this one) came from exactly that exception — a script written "just this once" to skip the harness because it seemed simple. ANY script that touches the network or the database — seeds, debug scripts, one-offs, ad hoc checks, everything — goes through `verify-harness.ts` (`step`/`withTimeout`/`runVerification`) from the first line, full stop. If a throwaway script feels too trivial to justify the harness's ceremony, that feeling is the failure mode, not a reason to skip it.
  - **Audit performed 2026-08-12 for any other unbounded network/DB call, production paths included:** every `createClient()` call site in the repo (`admin.server.ts`, `client.ts` — confirmed via grep, nothing else calls it per the CLAUDE.md rule) now has a fetch timeout. `provider.server.ts` (Twelve Data) already fixed 2026-08-11. `finnhub.server.ts`'s fetch is already routed through `ratelimit.server.ts`'s `withHardTimeout` (8s, from the H2 hardening pass) — already bounded. The two `new Anthropic(...)` client sites (`insights.server.ts`, `agent/anthropic.server.ts`) rely on the SDK's own default 10-minute timeout — bounded, not the same unbounded-hang class, left as-is. No `axios`/`XMLHttpRequest`/other HTTP client usage found anywhere in the app. **Conclusion: every network call in this codebase now has SOME finite ceiling** — the last remaining gap (this incident's actual trigger) is closed.
**🔐 Related discovery (M1):** `service_role` has NEVER had table-level UPDATE on `profiles` (0001 only granted it to `authenticated`) — all legitimate writes go through SECURITY DEFINER functions which run as the function owner. This is GOOD defense-in-depth (even the service key can't clobber money columns outside a vetted, event-logged function) and is deliberately preserved. Test-only seam added instead: `0013_margin_test_seed.sql` → `admin_seed_margin_state(user_id, margin_loan?, last_interest_accrued_at?)`, service_role-only, scoped to ONLY those 2 columns (never cash_balance, never margin_enabled/status). CTO-approved as not widening the attack surface (service_role can already move money via execute_trade); noted as a test seam living in prod. **Same root cause bit again inside product code, not just the verify script:** `lib/margin/monitor.server.ts` wrote `margin_status` via the same kind of raw, unchecked `profiles` write and had been silently failing since M1 shipped — fixed via a dedicated `set_margin_status` RPC (`0014_margin_set_status.sql`). That, plus the CTO-ordered codebase-wide audit for the whole unchecked-Supabase-write bug class, is written up in full in the Changelog below.

**⚙️ PROCESS: Venky added a `cto-rigor` skill (2026-06-26) — adopted as the operating standard.** Key upgrades to enforce: preflight env checks before builds; definition-of-done with proof on diverse/adversarial inputs (never single happy-path); no patch jobs (general fixes only, no hardcoded defaults); serverless-awareness (in-memory state doesn't survive Vercel invocations); verify deployed, not just local; never rubber-stamp CC's "done" — check every requirement individually; keep this HANDOFF living. First catch under the new standard: V1 Insights' per-stock day-cache was spec'd in-memory → would leak paid Claude calls on serverless; amended to persist in the 0009 `insights` table (kind='stock', symbol, unique per symbol+day, authenticated-readable since no user data).

**📉 EPIC FULLY COMPLETE + HARDENED — Options & Margin paper trading (kicked off 2026-08-01, closed 2026-08-08, hardening pass done 2026-08-08). Status: O1–O4 ✅ · M1 ✅ · M2 ✅ · hardening pass ✅ — all committed locally, NOT yet pushed (see CURRENT STATE at top).** Phase plan (CTO): **O1** pricing engine — pure Black-Scholes module + generated option chains (deterministic, unit-tested math; no DB/UI) → **O2** DB model + server-side buy/sell engine (long calls/puts, sell-to-close, premium×100 contract mechanics) → **O3** options UI (chain browser on Stock Detail, order flow, positions w/ P&L) → **O4** expiration/exercise processing (fold into daily cron) → **M1** margin engine (opt-in, 2× buying power, daily interest, maintenance + simulated margin calls) → **M2** margin UI + education → hardening/QA. Design locks from roadmap: model-derived premiums (disclosed), v1 = long calls/puts + sell-to-close only, educational explainers everywhere. Venky's ask 2026-07-23.
- ✅ **O1 (pricing engine) DONE + verified, committed locally — NOT pushed. No DB, no UI, no trading — pure foundations, per plan.** `lib/options/blackscholes.ts` (pure European call/put pricing + delta/gamma/theta/vega), `lib/options/volatility.server.ts` (realized-vol estimator, clamped 10%-150%), `lib/options/chain.server.ts` (generated expiries + strike ladder + priced contracts), `lib/options/functions.ts` (`getOptionChainFn`, JWT-verified, 1hr day-cached). Full detail + real numbers in the changelog entry below.
- ✅ **O2 (trade engine) DONE + verified against the REAL applied migration, committed locally — NOT pushed.** Migration `0010_options.sql` applied by Venky: `option_positions` (owner-only RLS select; writes exclusively via the SECURITY DEFINER function) + `option_transactions` (append-only ledger, same pattern as equities' `transactions`) + `execute_option_trade` (atomic, row-locked, `service_role`-only EXECUTE — the exact Phase 6 `execute_trade` pattern). `executeOptionTradeFn` (JWT-verified): the client sends ONLY `{contractId, side, contracts}` — the server re-derives symbol/type/strike/expiry from the contract id itself via a new `parseContractId`, rejects expired contracts (checked BOTH server-side and again in the SQL function, a deliberate double backstop), and computes the premium itself (live spot + realized vol → Black-Scholes via a new `priceParsedContract`) — there's no `premium` field anywhere in the input schema, so a client-doctored one gets silently stripped before the handler runs.
- ✅ **O3 (options UI) DONE + verified live end-to-end in the real running app, committed locally — NOT pushed.** New Options tab on Stock Detail (chain browser: expiry pills, strike-centered calls/puts table, ATM auto-highlighted, ITM shading, disclaimer + collapsible "What are options?" explainer), a responsive buy/sell order dialog (bottom-sheet on phone / centered dialog on desktop, one component via Tailwind breakpoints), positions shown identically on both the stock page and Portfolio (one shared `["optionPositions"]` React Query, server-computed via new `lib/options/valuation.server.ts` — true single source of truth), and option positions now fold into Dashboard's total portfolio value + today's-change AND the daily snapshot writer (closing both `TODO(O3)` markers from O2). Full real numbers + methodology notes in the changelog entry below.
- ✅ **O4 (expiration processing) DONE + verified against the REAL applied migration, committed locally — NOT pushed. The Robinhood-parity-style "options lifecycle" is now fully closed** — positions can no longer be left dangling past expiry with nothing happening. v1 = CASH SETTLEMENT only (no share delivery/exercise), disclosed in the explainer. Migration `0011_option_expiry.sql`: widened `option_transactions.side` to add `'expired'`/`'settled'` (two distinct values, not one side with total=0-vs->0, so the ledger self-documents which happened) + `settle_expired_option(p_user_id, p_contract_id, p_settle_per_share)` — same SECURITY DEFINER/service_role-only lockdown as every other money-moving function in this schema, reads `contracts` from the LOCKED position row itself (never a caller-supplied count), and rejects any position whose `expiry >= current_date` — structurally unable to touch a live position no matter what the caller's own query filtered for. New `lib/options/expiry.server.ts` `runExpiryProcessing()`: finds every position with `expiry < today` (the exact same boundary `execute_option_trade` already uses for "expired" — the two functions can never disagree about a contract's status; a contract expiring TODAY stays tradeable all day and settles on TOMORROW's run), batches one historical-candle fetch per distinct underlying (shared `getDailyHistory`), settles each via the underlying's close ON the expiry date (falls back to the latest available close if that exact date's candle is missing — documented, not silent), per-position error isolation, `onlyUserId` verification seam. Folded into the EXISTING `/api/cron/snapshot` endpoint (Vercel Hobby's 2-cron cap stays at 2 — same "fold in" pattern already used for the AI daily brief), running BEFORE the snapshot write so settlement cash lands in the SAME day's snapshot. UI: `OptionsExplainer` gained a 4th paragraph disclosing cash settlement; Portfolio's Options card gained a small "Recent option activity" list (buy/sell/settled/expired, clearly labeled — "Settled $478.00" vs "Expired worthless"); positions list needed no change (a settled position just disappears, same as a sell). Full real numbers in the changelog entry below.

**🎨 NEW EPIC — Robinhood-parity UX.** Venky wants the app to feel like Robinhood. CTO audit → prioritized, small verifiable phases: **R1** chart experience (per-range change readout "Past month: ±$X (±Y%)" + scrub/hover crosshair with live start→cursor recompute, on Stock Detail + shared PortfolioValueChart so dashboard + agent charts get it too; no new data fetching) → **R2** dollar-based/fractional buys ("Shares | Dollars" toggle; backend already stores numeric fractional qty — mostly UI + server fn accepting dollar amount) → **R3** stock-page story (real news feed tab from already-fetched Finnhub news, About section, richer position card) → **R4 mobile/tablet polish pass (added 2026-07-31, CTO-sequenced LAST in epic deliberately: R2 rebuilds the order panel + R3 rebuilds the stock page — the two most mobile-sensitive layouts — so we polish final layouts ONCE).** R4 scope from Venky's phone screenshot: stat cards should go 2-across on phones (currently full-width stack = a whole screen of scrolling), chart y-axis duplicate tick labels ("$100k/$100k/$99k/$99k" — k-rounding collision), holdings table clips horizontally, and a general every-page audit at phone + iPad widths. Note: R1 scrub CONFIRMED working on a real phone (Venky's screenshot shows touch tooltip + Selected readout) — touch caveat closed. Deliberately NOT copying: options/crypto/margin/cash/social. Deferred + flagged: real sparklines/top-movers on Markets rows (burns the tight Twelve Data ~8/min historical budget).
- ✅ **R1 DONE + verified, PUSHED to `origin/main` (part of `61f23c3`).** Post-deploy: Venky did a real-phone touch-drag check (confirmed working, see below). New pure module `lib/chartReadout.ts` → `computeRangeReadout(points, overrideStart?)`: first-vs-last change % + $ and low/high over a window (or a partial slice for scrubbing), all computed from series ALREADY loaded — no new fetches. `RANGE_LABEL` gives plain-word labels ("Past month", never "1M"). New `components/ChartReadout.tsx` → `RangeChangeReadout` (shared UI, same sign/color/format convention as the existing header day-change).
  - **Stock Detail (`LivePriceChart.tsx`):** now accepts an optional `quote` prop (StockDetail passes its ALREADY-fetched quote — no refetch) so the 1D readout reuses the header's own `dayChange`/`dayChangePct` numbers VERBATIM (guaranteed exact agreement, not just a close recompute) and the chart's right edge swaps the last 5-min bar for the live price. Non-1D ranges compute purely from the loaded candle series. Scrub: `onMouseMove`/`onMouseLeave` on the `AreaChart` (Recharts' own state — `activeTooltipIndex` — no custom hit-testing) drives a `hoverIdx`; while scrubbing the readout recomputes "start → cursor" (1D's start stays previous-close so intermediate points are still measured the honest way); on leave it reverts to the full-window readout. Crosshair = Recharts' existing dashed cursor line + a styled `activeDot`; the floating price/date tooltip is Recharts' own `Tooltip`, not custom UI.
  - **`PortfolioValueChart.tsx` (dashboard + agent, one shared component):** same `computeRangeReadout` + scrub wiring added directly to the existing chart (both the `v` and `spy` Areas got `activeDot`s), so the agent chart's Tooltip already shows BOTH the agent value and the S&P 500 (indexed) value at the cursor for free — verified live. Renders alongside (not replacing) the existing SPY beat/lag `BenchmarkReadout`.
  - 🐛 **Bonus fix (found via my own verification, not asked for, fixed because it undermined trust in the new readout):** `PortfolioValueChart`'s axis ticks + tooltip, and `LivePriceChart`'s tooltip for non-1D (daily-bar) ranges, formatted UTC-midnight date strings with `toLocaleDateString()`/`toLocaleString()` and no `timeZone: "UTC"` — same off-by-one bug class H4b already fixed in the Simulator (`SimulatorPanel.tsx`'s `prettyDate`), just never caught here. In timezones behind UTC (verified under the dev machine's real `America/Chicago`) a snapshot stored as `2026-07-26` displayed as "7/25/2026" in the tooltip — I caught it because a scrub tooltip showed a date that didn't match the value I'd seeded for it. Fixed by adding `timeZone: "UTC"` to both formatters (1D's intraday tooltip correctly stays LOCAL-time, since those bars carry a real time-of-day, not midnight). Dollar/percent math was never affected — only the displayed calendar date.
  - **Verified (real running dev app, throwaway test user `pt-r1-verify-…@example.org`, cleaned up after — 0 rows/user left in any table post-cleanup):** AAPL — 1D "Today: −$4.76 (−1.41%)" matches the header EXACTLY (both literally the same numbers); 3 scrub cursor spot-checks at 11:05/12:45/2:25 all matched `cursor − $338.19 (previous close)` to the cent; 1M "Past month: +$44.07 (+15.23%)", Range $289.36–$340.08 — independently recomputed from the raw candle payload (`first=289.35999, last=333.42999`), exact match. VOO — 3M "+$24.29 (+3.69%)", Range $654.24–$698.26 — independently recomputed via `providerCandles("VOO","3M")` outside the browser (`first=657.5, last=681.78998`), exact match. NVDA (volatile) — 3M "−$21.57 (−9.96%)", Range $190.01–$235.74 — independently recomputed the same way, exact match; 1D scrub spot-check (11:05 AM, $195.24 vs previous close $190.01) matched to the cent. Mouse-leave reverts to the full-window readout on every chart, confirmed. No NaN observed on any range/symbol including 0-holdings/empty states. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` clean, client-bundle grep → 0 matches for all 5 server-only secrets. - ✅ **R2 (dollar-based/fractional investing) DONE + verified, PUSHED to `origin/main` (`1004654`). No migration** — `holdings.quantity`/`avg_cost` and `transactions.quantity` were already unconstrained `numeric` (no scale limit), confirmed by reading `0002_portfolio_tables.sql`; `execute_trade`'s weighted-avg-cost SQL was already generically correct for any real quantity, verified by direct DB inspection rather than assumed.
  - **Order panel (`app.stock.$symbol.tsx`):** new Shares/Dollars segmented toggle (Robinhood-style, same visual pattern as the buy/sell toggle). Shares mode now allows fractional input (`step="any"`, no more `Math.floor`). Dollars mode shows a live "≈ N.NNNN shares @ $price" estimate as the user types, computed from the CLIENT's displayed price for UX only — the actual quantity executed is always resolved server-side from a fresh price, per rule #2. Client-side validation (`validateSharesQty`/`validateDollarAmount`) rejects zero/negative/absurd precision (>6dp for shares, >2dp for dollars) and enforces sensible minimums (≥0.0001 shares, ≥$1) with friendly toasts, mirroring the existing `insufficient_funds`/`insufficient_shares` error style.
  - **`executeTradeFn` (`lib/trading/functions.ts`):** input is now `quantity` XOR `amount` XOR `sellAll` (zod `.refine` enforces exactly one). For `amount` orders the SERVER computes `qty = amount / serverPrice`, rounded to 6dp — the client's estimate is never trusted for execution, only display. For a `sellAll` order the server reads the EXACT stored `holdings.quantity` (no rounding at all) and sells precisely that — zero dust, because it's literally the same DB value, not a recomputation. For a dollar-based SELL, the computed qty is capped at the held quantity (`Math.min(rawQty, held)`) so "sell $X worth" of a smaller position just closes it instead of erroring.
  - **"Sell all" affordance:** a toggleable button (shown only when `side==="sell"` and a position exists) reading "Sell all — closes your entire position (N shares ≈ $X)"; when active it hides the Shares/Dollars section entirely and the confirm button reads "Confirm sell · all SYMBOL (N.NNNNNN)".
  - **Display formatting:** new `fmtQty(n, maxDp=6)` in `lib/mockData.ts` (locale-formatted, trims trailing zeros — "1" not "1.000000", "0.241512" not truncated). Audited and fixed every raw `{h.quantity}`/`{t.quantity}` render app-wide: Stock Detail (position stat + recent trades), Dashboard holdings table, Portfolio holdings + transactions tables, Agent holdings table + decision-log trade lines. Confirmation toast now reads "Bought ≈0.2555 shares of NVDA for $50.00" in Dollars/sell-all mode, or "Bought 0.5 NVDA @ $195.47" in Shares mode.
  - **Rounding policy (stated, not hidden):** dollar-order quantities are rounded to 6 decimal places before execution, so the realized cost can differ from the requested dollar amount by a sub-cent fraction (observed: $50 requested → cost off by ~$0.0000093, invisible at the 2dp currency display everyone sees). Sell-all has NO such rounding — it uses the exact stored quantity, so a full-position close is always exactly zero dust.

Buy 0.5 shares (Shares mode, server re-fetched a fresh price of $195.83988802 at execution, different from the momentary display price — expected and correct) → position 0.755545 shares, weighted avg cost $195.77911931122568 — independently recomputed by hand from the two real fills and matched to 11 significant figures. Sell $25 worth → shares reduced to 0.628228, avg cost unchanged (correct — sells don't alter cost basis), buying power +$25.00 exactly. **Sell all** → position fully closed, "You don't own any NVDA yet" shown, **`holdings` table confirmed to have ZERO rows for that user+symbol** (not a zeroed row — actually deleted, true zero dust), cash reconciled to the cent ($100,000.4388815, matching a $0.44 net gain from real intra-test price movement, nothing unaccounted for). Rejections, all with NO balance change (confirmed via direct DB read after): overspend $500,000 → "Not enough buying power for this order."; $0.50 → "Minimum order is $1.00."; 0 shares → "Enter a quantity greater than zero."; −5 shares → same friendly message (not a crash); selling with a zero position → "You don't have enough shares to sell." `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` clean, client-bundle grep → 0 matches for secrets + `getServiceClient`/`execute_trade`/`verifyUser`.

Noted alongside the existing `insights`-DELETE and `holdings`-INSERT gaps for a future cleanup migration.
- ✅ **R3 (stock-page story) DONE + verified, SHIPPED to `origin/main` (part of the R4 push, `a3b4b93`). No migration/new provider** — everything reuses data already being fetched (Finnhub profile2, `fhCompanyNews`, existing holdings/quotes).
  - **News tab:** new `getCompanyNewsFn` (`lib/marketData/functions.ts`) exposes `fhCompanyNews(symbol, 7, 10)` — last 7 days, capped at 10. Also added `url` to `NewsItem` (Finnhub's article link was fetched but previously discarded) and moved `NewsItem` into the shared `types.ts` (matching how `Quote`/`SymbolMatch` are already organized there, rather than living only in `finnhub.server.ts`). **Behavior change, deliberate:** `fhCompanyNews` used to swallow ALL errors internally and always return `[]` — changed so errors now PROPAGATE, so the News tab (and only the News tab, via its own try/catch in the new server fn) can tell "genuinely no news" apart from "provider error" and show a different message for each. The two EXISTING callers (agent thinker, AI Insights) already wrapped every call in `.catch(() => [])` defensively, so this is backward-compatible — verified by reading both call sites before changing the shared function. Cache key now includes the requested `limit` (`fh:news:{sym}:{limit}`) since different callers request different limits (5/8/10) and were previously able to silently share/clobber each other's cached list.
  - **About tab:** rewritten into a real company card — logo, name, symbol + exchange, sector/industry, market cap, country, IPO date, "Visit website" (opens in a new tab). Extended Finnhub's `/stock/profile2` parsing (`fhProfile`) to also capture `weburl`/`country`/`ipo` — same API call already being made for name/sector/logo/market cap, zero extra cost. ETF degrade: when the profile is empty (no sector, no market cap — Finnhub's ETF behavior), shows "Exchange-traded fund" instead of a blank/NaN sector, and the IPO-date/website rows are simply omitted (not shown as dangling "—") when the data doesn't exist for a fund.
  - **Position card:** richer "Your position" — shares (`fmtQty`), avg cost, market value, today's $ change (`quote.dayChange × qty`), total return $ and % (vs. avg cost), and % of total portfolio. The portfolio-total denominator reuses the Dashboard's EXACT formula (`cash + Σ qty×price` across all holdings) — Stock Detail already had the current symbol's quote loaded, so it only fetches quotes for the user's OTHER held symbols (via the existing `useQuotes` hook) rather than refetching everything. Non-holders see a plain nudge card ("You don't own SYMBOL yet... place a paper order from the panel on the right").
  - **Tabs, not new page sections:** kept the existing tab-based design language (Your position / News / About / Recent trades — News and About inserted, trades moved last) rather than stacking new full-width cards, per the prompt's "your call, keep consistent" — chart and order panel untouched.
  - 🐛 **Real bug found + fixed during verification (my own new code, not a testing artifact):** `NewsTab` originally checked `newsQ.isLoading` to decide when to show the spinner. In React Query v5, `isLoading` = `isPending && isFetching`, which goes FALSE during the gap between a failed attempt and its retry (still pending, but not actively fetching) — during that gap, with `retry:1` on a forced-error test, the code fell through past both the loading and error checks straight to `items ?? []` and rendered "No recent news" for a symbol that was actually mid-retry, not actually empty. Fixed by checking `isPending` instead (stays true across retries, only clears once truly settled). Root-caused via a temporary forced-error edit to `getCompanyNewsFn` (reverted immediately after) rather than guessing — confirmed the fix by observing "Loading news…" persist correctly through a live retry cycle before reverting.
  - **VERIFIED end-to-end on the real running app** (throwaway test user `pt-r3-verify-…@example.org`, cleaned up after): AAPL — 10 real headlines with real sources (ChartMill, Yahoo) and relative times ("35m ago" → "2h ago"), links confirmed via DOM inspection to be real `finnhub.io/api/news?id=...` URLs with `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`; About card showed "Apple Inc · AAPL · NASDAQ NMS - GLOBAL MARKET", sector "Technology", market cap "$4.9T", country "US", IPO "1980-12-12", "Visit website" → confirmed `href="https://www.apple.com/"` with correct target/rel. Bought $50 of AAPL (0.165651 fractional shares) — position card math verified by hand from a single snapshot (price $301.31: market value $49.91, total return −$0.09/-0.18%, today's change −$5.32, all recomputed independently and matched exactly) — then cross-checked against the Dashboard loaded a few seconds later at the SAME live price: holdings-table market value $49.91, P&L −$0.09 (-0.18%) — **identical to the cent** on both pages. VOO — ETF degrade confirmed clean: "VANGUARD S&P 500 ETF", "Exchange-traded fund", market cap/country both "—" with no NaN or blank gaps, no dangling IPO/website rows; real news also present (SeekingAlpha, Yahoo sources). No-position nudge state confirmed on AAPL/VOO/TSLA/AMD before any purchase. Rate-limit/error path confirmed by a temporary forced-throw test (reverted): renders "Couldn't load this" + the exact friendly rate-limit message + a working "Try again" button. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` clean, client-bundle grep → 0 matches for all 5 secrets + `fhCompanyNews`/`fhGet`/`getServiceClient`.

**📰 NEW EPIC — AI Insights (in progress).** News-driven, history-aware buy/sell ANALYSIS (educational, not advice; prominent disclaimer; no fake "millions of traders" claims — Venky's original ask included crowd data we don't have; CTO pushed back, honest sourcing = live Finnhub news + market history + our own computed stats). Two stages, Venky confirmed order:
- **V1 (doing now):** per-stock "AI Insight" on Stock Detail (on-demand button, day-cached, one structured Claude call: lean/confidence/summary/drivers grounded in real news/historical_parallel/risks/watch_for) + daily "Market brief" dashboard card for holdings+watchlist (one Claude call/user/day, stored in new `insights` table, migration 0009; fold into EXISTING cron — Vercel Hobby 2-cron cap, both slots used). Placement/trigger/tone decided: stock page + daily brief · on-demand+cached · clear lean + disclaimer.
- **V1 status:** built; CTO review caught that CC missed the serverless-cache amendment (used in-memory cached() only) → CC owned it and reworked: corrected `0009_insights.sql` (user_id nullable, symbol column, kind check, PARTIAL unique indexes — one stock insight per symbol/day GLOBALLY shared, one brief per user/day; RLS: any authenticated user reads kind='stock', own-only for kind='brief'; includes repair path for earlier table shape; note: PostgREST upsert can't infer partial indexes → explicit insert-then-update helper `writeInsightRow`). `getStockInsight` is DB-FIRST (table check → Claude on miss → persist), cached() only a hot layer. ⚠️ Accepted residual (CTO call): same-second first-request race on one symbol can cost one duplicate Claude call — rare/bounded, advisory-lock fix deliberately not built. Migration `0009` applied by Venky 2026-07-31.
- **V1 VERIFICATION RESULTS (2026-07-31, real Claude + Finnhub + Supabase calls, no mocks):**
  - ✅ **Per-stock insight**, both symbols well-formed + news-grounded + zero directive-advice language: NVDA → `lean: bullish, confidence: moderate`, 5 drivers citing real headlines (Jensen Huang memory-bottleneck comments, Cathie Wood's $14.3M buy, FCC restrictions on Chinese AI hardware), historical_parallel framed as a general pattern not a prediction, `usedNews: 8`. VOO → `lean: neutral, confidence: moderate`, drivers grounded in real macro headlines (GDP, Core PCE, Fed posture), `usedNews: 8`.
  - ✅ **Three Claude-call counters, all as specified:** first NVDA call → **1**. Same-process repeat of `getStockInsight("NVDA")` → **0 extra** (total stayed 1), `generatedAt` identical, confirming the in-process `cached()` fast path. **Fresh, separate node process** (genuinely empty module memory — real cold-start simulation) reading NVDA back → **0 Claude calls**, `generatedAt: 2026-07-31T06:40:39.791Z` — bit-for-bit identical to the original generation, proving it was served durably from the `insights` table, not regenerated.
  - ✅ **Daily brief: VERIFIED end-to-end, including rendered in the browser.** Real root cause found (not the Postgres/PostgREST issue first suspected): the brief job's watchlist-seeding step in the VERIFICATION HARNESS used `.upsert(rows, { onConflict: "user_id,symbol" })` — a plain upsert defaults to `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`, which Postgres requires `UPDATE` privilege for (checked at parse time, regardless of whether a row actually conflicts) — and `authenticated` has never had `UPDATE` on `watchlist` (confirmed via `information_schema.role_table_grants`: has SELECT/INSERT/DELETE, not UPDATE). The REAL app's `addToWatchlist()` (`lib/portfolio/queries.ts`) was never affected — it passes `ignoreDuplicates: true`, which compiles to `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` and only needs INSERT. **`NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema'` was a red herring** (harmless, but not the fix) — this was never a stale cache. Fixed `verify-insights.ts`'s seeding line to match the real app's `ignoreDuplicates: true`; brief generation then passed immediately: 1 Claude call, `briefsWritten: 1`, stored row (`kind='brief'`, `symbol: null`, `user_id` set) with a real headline + 3 grounded items (NVDA/AAPL/VOO) + disclaimer, and **confirmed rendering live in the browser** (Dashboard → "Today's market brief" card, screenshot-verified, content matching the DB row exactly).
  - 🐛 **Bug found + fixed during this render check:** `MarketBriefBody`'s footer showed "For Jul 30" for a brief stored as `created_at: "2026-07-31"` — the same date-only/UTC-midnight-vs-local-render bug already fixed for the simulator in H4b, just not applied here. Fixed in `components/InsightUI.tsx` (added `timeZone: "UTC"` to the `toLocaleDateString` call); re-verified live, now correctly shows "For Jul 31". `generatedAt` (used on the per-stock insight card) is a full timestamp, not date-only, so it wasn't affected.
  - 🔧 **Also found, separate, minor, not yet fixed:** `service_role` has no `DELETE` grant on `insights` (0009 granted only select/insert/update) — only matters to test-harness cleanup today (silently caught there), but is a real gap worth a follow-up one-line grant whenever 0009 (or a 0010) is next touched. Also: `service_role` has never had `INSERT` on `holdings` either (only SELECT, since Phase 6/8) — not a bug, just noted since it surfaced during this investigation.
  - **V1 is now FULLY VERIFIED** (per-stock insight + daily brief, both with real Claude output, correct storage, and confirmed rendering). Committed locally across two commits (`AI Insights V1` feature + a follow-up fix for the harness/date-bug — see changelog), **NOT pushed** per standing default. Migration `0009` applied.
- ✅ **V2 (event study) DONE + verified, committed locally — NOT pushed. No new provider/migration.** Solved the "hard part" (defining a comparable event) by keying off the stock's OWN price history rather than news-event matching: a "shock" is a single-day return whose magnitude exceeds 2× the standard deviation of its own trailing 60-day returns (computed strictly before that day — no look-ahead), split into up-shocks/down-shocks. For each qualifying shock (only counted once its +1-month forward window is fully realized in the data — no partial/future events), measures the +1-week and +1-month forward returns, then aggregates avg/median/worst/best/%-positive. Full detail + real verification numbers in the changelog entry below.

**(prior epic note) AI Portfolio Agent —** An autonomous AI "personal investor" managing a SEPARATE virtual sub-portfolio (user funds it with a chosen amount from their virtual cash). Decisions locked: fully autonomous + guardrails · hybrid brain = quant rules + LLM news reading (needs **ANTHROPIC_API_KEY**, server-only) · two-loop cadence (cheap intraday "watchdog" for trailing stops/guardrails + ~daily LLM "thinker") · smart volatility-sized trailing stops + rebalance (explicitly NOT naive 1% stops — whipsaw risk explained to Venky) · educational SIMULATION, paper money only, NOT financial advice (UI disclaimer required) · no guarantee of gains (set honestly with Venky). Sub-phases 10.1–10.5 in ROADMAP.md. Separate agent sub-portfolio chosen so the agent never disturbs the user's manual holdings and its performance is cleanly trackable.

**10.1 (foundations) SHIPPED to `origin/main` (`5d2388d`), auto-redeployed live.** Migration `0005_agent.sql` applied (agent_config/agent_holdings/agent_transactions/agent_decisions, owner-only RLS; `fund_agent(user_id, amount)` atomic main↔agent cash move, service_role-only). `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` stored server-only (unused until 10.2). New `/app/agent` page (Bot nav icon): disclaimer banner, Activate toggle, risk selector (conservative/balanced/aggressive), mode (autonomous/approve), Fund/Withdraw with live balances, empty states. JWT-verified server fns getAgentConfig/updateAgentConfig/fundAgent. Verified: FUND +5k → main 95k/agent 5k; WITHDRAW −2k → main 97k/agent 3k; over/zero rejected; risk persists across reload; keys absent from client bundle.

**10.2 (decision engine — the "thinker") DONE + end-to-end verified, committed (NOT pushed — Venky reviews executed portfolios first).** Migration `0006` applied. Hybrid brain, all server-only:
- QUANT (`lib/agent/quant.server.ts`): scores universe (8 majors + SPY/QQQ/VOO) from Finnhub quote + `/stock/metric` (momentum=blended 13w/26w return, beta, 52-wk position) → z-scored, risk-weighted formula. Aggressive favors momentum+beta; conservative punishes beta + rewards ETFs. Added `fhMetrics` + `fhCompanyNews` to `finnhub.server.ts` (cached). No Twelve Data — avoids its 8 req/min limit.
- AI (`lib/agent/anthropic.server.ts`): ONE structured-output call to Claude (`@anthropic-ai/sdk`), JSON-schema constrained (`output_config.format`), prompt-cached system. Reads shortlist signals + recent news → per-symbol {include, weight_hint, reason} + commentary. Chooses ONLY from shortlist; graceful fallback to quant-only on any AI error. **Model configurable via server-only `AGENT_MODEL` env (no VITE_); defaults to `claude-sonnet-4-6`** (cost — one call/run; set to claude-opus-4-8 for max reasoning). `agentModel()` helper.
- Guardrails+execution (`lib/agent/thinker.server.ts`): cash buffer + max single position + min/max holdings per risk (cons .25/.25/5–7 · bal .15/.30/4–6 · agg .08/.35/3–5); normalize→cap per-position→renormalize; buys into agent via new SECURITY DEFINER `agent_execute_trade` (migration `0006`, mirrors execute_trade on agent_cash/agent_holdings/agent_transactions); logs per-buy + overall rebalance `agent_decisions`; never overspends agent_cash. `runAgentThinkerFn` (JWT) + "Run agent now" button on /app/agent (enabled when activated+funded).
- VERIFIED END-TO-END on `claude-sonnet-4-6` (real `runThinker` via vite-node, $50k funded test agent per risk level, then DB read-back): ALL guardrails held at every level — max single position (cons 17.8%≤25 · bal 19.2%≤30 · agg 27.6%≤35), cash buffer (27.3% · 16.9% · 8.8%), holdings count (5∈[5,7] · 6∈[4,6] · 5∈[3,5]), agent_cash never negative, spent==invested exactly, every holding has a buy txn. Decision quality strong + risk-differentiated on Sonnet (matches Opus preview): conservative ETF-anchored (VOO core + cash buffer, excludes AMD/GOOGL/META), aggressive tilts to high-beta semis (AMD 27.6%+NVDA 21.7% ≈ half the book). typecheck+build clean; AGENT_MODEL + ANTHROPIC key/SDK + `agent_execute_trade` absent from client bundle (0 matches).

Now topped-up names get the average included weight + a clear "added for diversification/min-holdings" rationale (not Claude's exclude reason).

Currently /app/agent shows only counts ("5 positions / 6 decisions"); the breakdown UI is 10.4.

**10.3 (risk "watchdog" — protective trailing stops) DONE + rigorously verified, committed (NOT pushed — Venky reviews stop logic + anti-whipsaw first).** No new migration (reuses existing `agent_holdings.trailing_stop_price` + `agent_execute_trade` sell side). The CHEAP/FREQUENT loop, all server-only, NO LLM calls:
- `lib/agent/watchdog.server.ts` — `runWatchdog(userId, sources?)`. Per holding: fetch live price (held symbols only) + beta, RATCHET the trailing stop UP only (`stop = max(existing, price×(1−stopPct))` → implicitly tracks the high-water mark), and on breach (price ≤ stop) execute ONE protective SELL of the whole position via `agent_execute_trade` (proceeds → agent_cash, stay in agent; NO auto-rebuy — re-entry is the thinker's deliberate call). Logs each sell + a run-summary to `agent_decisions`; quiet ratchets (counted, not per-row logged).
- SMART anti-whipsaw: a dip that doesn't breach the (only-ever-rising) stop does nothing. `stopPct` is VOLATILITY-SIZED + risk-varied: `clamp(base + betaSlope·(β−1), min, max)` with cons {.08,.04,[.06,.18]} · bal {.12,.05,[.09,.25]} · agg {.18,.06,[.12,.35]}. Widths e.g. β1→cons8%/bal12%/agg18%; β2.5→cons14%/bal19.5%/agg27%. Higher beta = wider stop; aggressive widest (let winners run); conservative tightest. `stopPct()` exported + reused by the thinker to SEED the stop at buy time (`price×(1−stopPct)`).
- `runAgentWatchdogFn` (JWT) + "Run watchdog" button on /app/agent (enabled when activated + holds positions). Dependency-injected price/beta sources so it's deterministically testable.
- VERIFIED (deterministic stubbed price paths via vite-node — ALL PASSED): stop ratchets UP as price rises and NEVER moves down; a non-breaching dip → NO sell (anti-whipsaw); a real drawdown breach → exactly ONE sell, full proceeds to agent_cash, position closed, decision logged with correct peak ($120) + drawdown (−13.3%); high-β name gets a measurably WIDER stop than a calm one (β2.5 stop $80.5 < β0.8 $89) + aggressive wider than conservative; missing live quote → skipped + logged, never a phantom sell, no crash; agent_cash/shares stay consistent and non-negative. (Live-data smoke can't run under vite-node — `getQuotes` needs the TanStack Start server runtime; that path is the same one prod already uses + runs inside the real server fn.) typecheck + build clean; no secrets/server symbols in client bundle (0 matches).
- ⏳ NEXT: Venky reviews stop logic + anti-whipsaw, then push. Then 10.4 dashboard (surface holdings w/ stop levels + buys/sells log) → 10.5 scheduling.

SPY overlay deferred (TODO in UI). Snapshot writer (Phase 8 runSnapshots) extended to also record agent value daily into agent_snapshots; added `runSnapshots({onlyUserId})` for scoped runs. Verified end-to-end ($50k balanced agent, 11 checks): summary reconciles (cash $8,651.41 + invested $41,348.59 = $50k, weights 100%), all 6 holdings seeded stops + cushion, decision log shows 6 buys + 1 rebalance, agent_snapshots today row reconciles. Keys absent from client bundle. **NEXT: 10.5 scheduling (watchdog frequent + thinker ~daily via cron), then final hardening/QA pass.**

Smooth out (throttle/batch/cache, or chunk users) in the hardening pass. Also: SPY overlay on agent perf chart; live-render check of /app/agent dashboard; the earlier deferred cosmetics (README w/ live URL, prune test users, simulator timezone date fix). `lib/agent/watchdog.server.ts` + `runAgentWatchdogFn` + "Run watchdog" button (enabled when active + holds positions). No migration (reuses `agent_holdings.trailing_stop_price` + `agent_execute_trade` sell side). Trailing stop = max(existing, price×(1−stopPct)) — ratchets UP only, sells one whole position on breach, proceeds → agent_cash (no auto-rebuy; re-entry = thinker). `stopPct = clamp(base + betaSlope·(β−1), min, max)`, varies by risk (cons tightest/agg widest); β2.5→cons14%/bal19.5%/agg27%, β1.0→8/12/18. Same `stopPct()` seeds the stop at BUY time in the thinker. Verified (deterministic stubbed price paths): ratchet up-never-down, small dip→NO sell (anti-whipsaw), real breach→exactly one sell w/ proceeds + logged rationale, high-β wider than low-β, missing quote skipped (no phantom sell/crash), no negative cash/shares. Keys/server symbols absent from client bundle. Caveat: live `getQuotes` path can't run under vite-node harness (needs TanStack server runtime) — same path used in prod elsewhere; worth ONE live "Run watchdog" sanity check after deploy.
- 10.3 design locked: volatility-sized TRAILING stops that only ratchet UP (sell only on real drawdown from peak — NOT naive 1% dips), wider stops for high-beta names, vary by risk level; protective sells via agent_execute_trade → proceeds to agent_cash; NO auto-rebuy (re-entry is the thinker's deliberate call) — all to avoid whipsaw. Cheap loop, no LLM, just quotes for held symbols. Reminder: any NEW env var added locally must also be set in Vercel + redeploy (learned w/ ANTHROPIC_API_KEY).

**10.5 (autopilot scheduling — LAST BUILD PHASE of the agent epic) DONE + verified, committed (NOT pushed — Venky reviews schedule + auth, then push + sets GitHub secrets).** The agent runs itself; no new env/migration. No-ops cheaply when US market closed (`isUsMarketOpen()` — Mon–Fri 9:30–16:00 ET, DST-aware via Intl America/New_York; holidays not excluded).
- `lib/agent/cron.server.ts`: `runThinkerForAllAgents` / `runWatchdogForAllAgents` (+ handlers), per-agent try/catch (one failure can't abort the batch), agents run SEQUENTIALLY (natural Finnhub pacing), JSON summary {eligible, processed, trades/sells, errors}. Optional `{onlyUserId, sources, skipMarketCheck}` seams for safe scoped verification (prod calls with none). Wired in `app/src/server.ts` (dynamic import, like snapshot).

Workflow YAML valid (js-yaml). typecheck + build clean; no secrets committed (workflow reads GitHub secrets; 0 literal-secret matches); server-only symbols absent from client bundle.

**HARDENING #1 (gentler rebalancing — reduce churn) DONE + verified, committed (NOT pushed — Venky reviews, then push). No migration.** The thinker used to re-buy toward exact target weights every run (high turnover, and it ignored existing holdings entirely). Now it computes the MINIMAL trade set:
- NEW pure module `lib/agent/rebalance.ts` → `planRebalance(input)`. Constants: `DRIFT_BAND = 0.05` (±5pp of total capital, absolute & explainable), `COOLDOWN_DAYS = 4` (covers ~3 trading days incl. a weekend).
  - (1) DRIFT BAND / hysteresis: a TARGET position is only traded when its weight deviates from target by >5pp; within band → left alone (kills daily micro-churn).
  - (2) PREFER CASH over selling: underweight targets are filled with available agent_cash first (best quant score first); a held name is only TRIMMED if it's >5pp OVER target, and a name no longer in the target set is fully EXITED.
  - (3) COOLDOWN: symbols the watchdog protective-sold within COOLDOWN_DAYS are not rebought. Inferred from `agent_decisions` where `action='sell'` (watchdog logs 'sell'; thinker trims/exits now log `action='trim'`, so 'sell' = watchdog only — NO new table/column needed). Skipped picks are LOGGED (`action='hold'`, "re-entry cooldown").
  - Guardrails enforced inside the planner: never spend below the cash buffer, never exceed max-position cap, never overspend agent_cash.
- `thinker.server.ts` rewired: reads current holdings + live prices, totalCapital = agent_cash + Σ(qty×price), filters cooldown out of targets (capturing dropped AI picks), calls `planRebalance`, executes (sells first then buys), seeds the trailing stop ONLY for brand-new positions (never lowers a ratcheted stop on an add). Rebalance decision rationale: "Portfolio within drift bands — no trades needed." when nothing material, else Claude commentary + "Adjusted N; held M within ±5pp band". New `runThinker(userId, { disableAi })` test seam (deterministic quant-only). Dashboard: new `trim`/`hold` decision-badge styling; run toast reflects trades vs no-op.
- VERIFIED (vite-node — 20 checks ✅): PURE planRebalance — at-target → 0 trades, small drift (<band) → 0 trades (hysteresis), large drift → trims ONLY the drifted name (others held), new cash → buys only (no winners sold) w/ buffer respected, cooldown symbol skipped+reported while others proceed, non-target fully exited, max-position cap held (27.8%≤30%), no overspend, cash accounting balances. INTEGRATION on the REAL thinker (quant-only) — run twice: run1 4 trades → **run2 0 trades** (low turnover proven; old behavior would re-trade all); a pre-seeded watchdog stop-sell of AMD → AMD on cooldown, NOT rebought (agent built [QQQ,VOO,SPY,AAPL] instead); agent_cash never negative. typecheck + build clean; no secrets/server symbols in client bundle (0).
- 🔭 Possible follow-up (not done): membership churn — a name oscillating in/out of the target set across runs still causes exit/re-buy (the band addresses WEIGHT noise, not membership noise). Consider a stickiness/hysteresis on set membership if it shows up in practice.

**HARDENING #2 (batch jobs reliable under the Finnhub rate limit) DONE + verified, committed (NOT pushed — Venky reviews, then push). No migration/env.** Unattended batches (snapshot writer + agent thinker/watchdog crons) used to price held symbols PER USER → as users grow they'd blow Finnhub's 60/min (the silent slow/hung run we saw in 10.4 testing). Fixed two ways, behavior identical:
- GLOBAL limiter + retry + timeout (`lib/marketData/ratelimit.server.ts`, wired into `finnhub.server.ts` `fhGet`): one module-level `RateLimiter(50/min, 6 concurrent)` governs EVERY Finnhub call (sliding window: bursts to 50 then spaces). Per-call 8s `withTimeout` (AbortController) so a stuck request can't hang. `withRetry` = 3 retries, exp backoff 500→1000→2000 + jitter, retries only 429/5xx/network/timeout — NEVER a 4xx (bad symbol fails fast). All clock-injectable → deterministically tested. Added `fetchStats()`/`resetFetchStats()` test counters.
- Per-run DEDUP: thinker batch fetches ONE universe snapshot up front (`prefetchUniverse()` in quant.server.ts) and threads it through `runThinker(uid, { prefetch })` → `scoreCandidates(risk, prefetched)` for every agent (data is identical across agents; only scoring differs). Watchdog batch fetches the UNION of held symbols once and passes a shared `{prices,betas}` source to each `runWatchdog`. Snapshot writer already deduped. Added `onlyUserIds[]` scoping seam to both cron batches (verification only — never touches prod agents).
- VERIFIED (vite-node — 17 checks ✅): PURE (mock clock) — window throttle (first 50 instant, 51st waits a full minute), concurrency capped at 4, retry-429-then-succeed, give-up-after-4-attempts (no hang), no-retry on 4xx, backoff 500→1000→2000. INTEGRATION (real Finnhub fetch counters) — thinker prefetch: 1 snapshot = 11 quote fetches, 5 reuse runs add **0** extra (vs old 33 for 3 runs); scoring output identical with/without prefetch (AMD top both). Watchdog batch: **25 agents × 11 holdings → 11 quote fetches (NOT 275)**, all 25 processed, 0 errors. Resilience: one invalid symbol → placeholder (price 0), the whole `providerQuotes` call still resolves (Promise.allSettled — no abort/hang). typecheck + build clean; no secrets/server symbols in client bundle (0).
- 🔭 Next lever if still needed at scale (NOT built): durable Postgres `price_cache` (deferred since Phase 5) so the snapshot+cron quotes survive across serverless invocations.

**HARDENING #3 (S&P 500 benchmark on the agent performance chart) DONE + verified, committed (NOT pushed — Venky reviews, then push). No migration/env.** Closes the 10.4 TODO. The agent perf chart now overlays "same money in the S&P 500" so the user can see beat/lag vs the market.
- Data: reuses the existing `getCandles("SPY", "1Y")` (Twelve Data, same path as the What-If Simulator — NO new provider). Fetched once per chart load on /app/agent (react-query, 1h staleTime, retry 1, enabled only when ≥1 snapshot); the chart slices it locally per range toggle, so range changes don't refetch.
- Normalization (documented): BOTH lines indexed to the same start at the window's LEFT edge — `spyLine(d) = agentValue(firstVisibleDate) × spyClose(d)/spyClose(d0)`. So both start equal and the right-edge gap IS the out/under-performance. Limitation noted in code: mid-window deposits/withdrawals make the absolute-$ compare approximate — the return-% readout is the honest headline. SPY closes forward-filled across weekends/holidays.
- Pure math in `components/portfolioBenchmark.ts` → `indexBenchmark(points, spy)` (unit-tested, no React). Chart (`PortfolioValueChart`) gained an OPTIONAL `benchmark` prop (dashed muted 2nd Area + legend + beat/lag readout: "Agent +X% · S&P 500 +Y% · Beating/Lagging the market by Z%"). Opt-in, so the MAIN dashboard chart is unchanged (passes no benchmark). Recomputes per range toggle.
- Graceful: short history (<2 pts) → agent placeholder + "benchmark builds as history grows"; SPY fetch error/empty → agent line alone + quiet "benchmark unavailable"; never crashes the chart.
- VERIFIED: pure `indexBenchmark` (vite-node, 15 checks ✅) — SPY line starts exactly at agent's first value (same-start indexing); +20% agent / +21% S&P → "lagging by 1%"; +30% agent → "beating by 9%"; per-window re-index (2-day window re-bases to that window's start, +9.1% vs +10%); weekend forward-fill (missing SPY day stays flat); fallbacks (1 point / empty SPY / window-before-SPY-history) all return cleanly. SPY data source confirmed live: `providerCandles("SPY","1Y")` → 252 daily closes ($606.78 → $733.58). typecheck + build clean; no secrets in client bundle (0). NOTE: visual render is build-verified, not browser-rendered (the page is auth-gated + needs snapshot history; same precedent as 10.4's data-layer verification) — the math + data feed + fallbacks are proven by the pure suite.

**HARDENING #4a (approve-first mode actually works) DONE + verified, committed (NOT pushed — Venky reviews, then push). Migration `0008_agent_proposals.sql` applied.** Previously approve-mode agents were skipped and never proposed anything. Now the thinker PROPOSES for approve-mode agents (manual button + daily cron); the user approves/rejects; autonomous mode is unchanged.
- NEW `agent_proposals` table (id, user_id, created_at, status[pending|approved|rejected|superseded], target jsonb [{symbol,weight,score,beta,reason}], trades jsonb [human-readable buy/sell/trim], rationale, commentary; owner-only RLS select, service_role write; index (user_id,status,created_at desc)). Migration `0008`.
- Refactor: extracted `lib/agent/execute.server.ts` (GUARDRAILS, round2, `executePlan`) — shared by the autonomous thinker AND the approve executor so BOTH enforce identical guardrails (also breaks a thinker↔proposals import cycle). `lib/agent/proposals.server.ts`: `writeProposal` (supersedes prior pending), `executeProposal` (approve), `rejectProposal`, `supersedePending`.
- Thinker now reads `cfg.mode`: approve → compute the SAME target + planRebalance, write a pending proposal (storing target + indicative trades + Claude commentary), return `{proposed:true, executed:[]}` — NO trades. Autonomous → `executePlan` as before. `runThinker(uid,{disableAi})` test seam unchanged.
- APPROVE = JWT server fn `approveAgentProposalFn` → `executeProposal`: re-fetches CURRENT live prices (never stale proposal-time prices), re-applies the watchdog cooldown, renormalizes weights over allowed targets, runs planRebalance (all guardrails re-validated), executes (sells→buys, seeds stops for new positions via stored beta), marks 'approved', logs buy/trim decisions + an "approved proposal" rebalance entry. REJECT = `rejectAgentProposalFn` → marks 'rejected', executes nothing. Switching mode→autonomous supersedes any pending proposal (no stranded state).
- Cron `runThinkerForAllAgents` now selects BOTH modes (dropped the `mode='autonomous'` filter) + reports `proposalsTotal`. UI /app/agent: "Proposed changes" card (trade list w/ symbol+qty+price, rationale, commentary, Approve & Reject) shown when mode='approve' + a pending proposal; run button relabels to "Propose changes"; toasts cover propose/approve/reject. Approve-mode helper text added.
- VERIFIED END-TO-END ($50k balanced test agents, real thinker + executor, quant-only — 22 checks ✅): approve run → proposed=true, 0 trades, 0 holdings, exactly 1 pending (4 targets/4 trades); 2nd run → 1 superseded + 1 new pending; APPROVE → 4 trades at current prices, agent_cash $50,000→$9,177.41, spent==invested ($50k), max position 20.7%≤30% cap, stops seeded, buys + approval-rebalance decisions logged; REJECT → 'rejected', 0 holdings/trades; AUTONOMOUS → 4 trades immediately, holdings created, NO proposal. typecheck + build clean; client bundle has no server symbols/secrets (0; the lone case-insensitive hit was the UI word "guardrails").
- 🔭 Follow-up (4b, not built): a proposal expiry/"propose again" affordance + showing approved/rejected history; approve currently uses an empty-jitter equal default if Claude weights are absent (quant-only) — fine.

Used in the TopBar dropdown AND on every Markets row (Popular grid + live search results); membership/filled-star state stays consistent across all three places.

- VERIFIED in the running dev app (preview, real Finnhub + real Supabase writes): top-bar search found QQQ ("INVESCO QQQ TRUST SERIES 1"), VOO, NFLX ("NETFLIX INC") with names; clicking a result opened its detail page (`/app/stock/NFLX`); the dropdown star added/removed symbols that then showed on the Watchlist tab; Markets rows show filled stars for already-tracked names (MSFT) and outline for others, and toggling works there too; the Watchlist tab's own remove (X) still works (not regressed). typecheck + build clean; 0 secrets in client bundle. (Test watchlist state + the throwaway login user were cleaned up afterward; `.claude/launch.json` preview config is gitignored.)
- ⚠️ Migration gotcha learned: don't relay abbreviated SQL in chat — apply migrations from the actual repo file (`supabase/migrations/*.sql`) or a verified-complete block; a partial copy created the function but not the tables (twice).

**Phase 9 build/deploy facts (important):** Lovable's vite config defaulted to a NON-Vercel build (Nitro→Cloudflare) which would break on Vercel — FIXED by pinning `nitro: { preset: "vercel" }` in `app/vite.config.ts` (build now emits `.vercel/output` Build Output API v3; SSR + server fns + `/api/cron/snapshot` all run as the `__server` function). **`vercel.json` lives at `app/vercel.json`** (NOT repo root) because Vercel Root Directory = `app/` — a root-level vercel.json would be ignored and the cron would never register. Cron: `{ "crons": [{ "path": "/api/cron/snapshot", "schedule": "0 22 * * *" }] }`. `.vercel/` gitignored. `DEPLOYMENT.md` runbook at repo root has the full click-by-click. Vercel auto-sends `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>` to cron when `CRON_SECRET` env is set (matches our endpoint).

**Phase 9 launch decisions (Venky, 2026-06-16):** (1) SOFT LAUNCH — live online but link shared selectively; current free data tiers are fine (full public launch deferred until we add the durable Postgres price_cache + likely paid data tiers, since free Finnhub/Twelve Data limits are shared across all users). (2) Free Vercel URL (e.g. `*.vercel.app`); custom domain later. (3) GitHub repo stays PRIVATE. Hosting: Vercel (frontend + cron) + existing Supabase project as prod (it's already cloud). Vercel Cron hits `/api/cron/snapshot` daily — Vercel auto-sends `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>` when `CRON_SECRET` env is set, which matches our endpoint. Must add the Vercel URL to Supabase Auth Site URL/redirects, and turn email confirmation back ON for launch.

**Money formulas (single source of truth, in dashboard code):** total_value = cash + Σ(qty×live price); today's Δ$ = Σ(qty×dayChange) [Finnhub change-vs-prior-close]; today's Δ% = todayΔ$/(total_value−todayΔ$); total return $ = total_value − 100000; total return % = (total_value−100000)/100000×100.

**Phase 8 scheduling decision (CTO, 2026-06-16):** daily `portfolio_snapshots` writer runs as a server function triggered by **Vercel Cron** (built-in once deployed in Phase 9) — fits the "server functions + server-only keys" pattern, zero extra infra. In Phase 8 we build the writer + migration + a token-protected manual trigger and test it by hand; the automatic daily schedule gets wired at deploy (Phase 9). Initial baseline seeded so the dashboard value chart isn't empty; reconstructing historical value from the transaction ledger + historical prices noted as an optional later enhancement.

**Phases done:** 0 (planning) ✅ · 1 (Lovable frontend) ✅ · 2 (monorepo) ✅ · 3 (auth, $100k) ✅ · 4 (portfolio tables + RLS) ✅ · 5 (live market data) ✅ · 6 (trading engine) ✅ · 7 (What-If Simulator) ✅ · hybrid data layer ✅ — all on `origin/main`. Phase 8 (snapshots/value chart/day-change) ✅ implemented + verified, pending push. **Phase 9 (launch) is the only one left.**

Both keys verified absent from client bundle. Verified: `git push origin main` succeeded, local HEAD == `origin/main` HEAD (`b553782`), `git push --dry-run` → "Everything up-to-date". If this breaks again, check `~/.gitconfig`'s credential helper path first before assuming a real auth/permissions problem.

Email confirmation turned OFF for dev testing (turn back ON before launch). URL + publishable key live in `app/.env` (gitignored, never pushed).

## 1. The people & the working model

- **Venky** (venkatpraveen1@gmail.com) — product owner / founder. Driving the project, makes final calls. Prefers concise, direct communication. Is not coding much himself by choice — wants the agent to do most of the coding.
- **Claude in Cowork** — acting **CTO + product manager**. Plans architecture, makes technical decisions, and writes precise prompts for Venky to run elsewhere.
- **Claude Code** — the **implementation agent**, connected to this folder (`Paper Trading - Venk` on Venky's desktop). Does the actual coding.
- **Lovable** — AI app builder generating the React frontend design.

**The core workflow loop:**
1. Cowork (CTO) and Venky plan together.
2. Cowork writes a precise, copy-paste-ready prompt.
3. Venky pastes it into **Lovable** (for frontend/design) or **Claude Code** (for implementation).
4. Venky brings the result back (screenshot, error, "it worked," or "this looks thin").
5. We iterate in small, tight loops.

Venky explicitly asked: "be my CTO and my product person."

**Standing communication rule (always follow this):** Venky is a product person, NOT technical. Every time he pastes back output from Claude Code, the CTO (Cowork) must respond in two clearly separated parts:
1. **"What just happened"** — plain English, product perspective. What was built/changed, what it means for the app and the user, any problems or decisions needed, whether we're on track. No technical jargon.
2. **"Your next prompt"** — the fully technical, specific, copy-paste-ready prompt for Claude Code.
Never make Venky parse raw technical output himself.

---

## 2. What we're building (one paragraph)

**PaperTrader** — a full-stack paper-trading web app. Users get $100,000 of virtual money, trade real stock tickers at live market prices (no real money), track a live portfolio with profit/loss, and run **"what if I had invested in this stock back then"** simulations against real historical data. It's a simulation / education tool — no real brokerage, no real money movement. The **What-If Simulator** is the flagship differentiator (most paper-trading apps only trade forward; ours looks backward too).

Full detail in `README.md`.

---

## 3. Decisions locked so far (with reasoning)

| Decision | Choice | Why |
|----------|--------|-----|
| Scope of v1 | Comprehensive full platform (not just a calculator) | Venky wants something "comprehensive" / full-fledged. |
| Data needs | Live **+** historical prices | Venky chose this explicitly. Needs a feed with both. |
| Platform | Web app | CTO pick; most flexible, easy to share/deploy. |
| Venky's involvement | Agent does most coding | Venky's choice; keep prompts explicit and beginner-safe. |
| Frontend stack | React + **TanStack Start** + TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Recharts | What Lovable actually generated (full-stack React w/ a server layer, NOT a plain Vite SPA). |
| Backend | **Supabase** (Postgres + Auth + RLS) for DB/auth; **TanStack Start server functions** for server-side logic | Supabase one-click from Lovable. Server logic (market data, trades) runs in TanStack server functions, not Supabase Edge Functions — simpler for a solo founder (one codebase, shared types, one deploy). |
| Market data | **Twelve Data primary** (key in use), **Finnhub** fallback (thin) | Both free tiers have live + historical. Isolated behind `lib/marketData/`. Twelve Data free ≈ 8 credits/min → small universe + TTL cache. |
| Source control | GitHub (`venk-cc-first-project`, monorepo) | Repo of record. Lovable sync is dead post-import; Claude Code is source of truth. |
| Hosting (planned) | Vercel (frontend) + Supabase cloud (backend) | Cheap/free to start. |

**Hard architectural rules (non-negotiable):**
1. Never call the market API from the browser — all market data via **TanStack Start server functions**; API key in a SERVER-ONLY env var (no `VITE_`), never in repo.
2. Never trust client-supplied prices/balances — trades execute server-side against a server-fetched price; user identity from the verified JWT, never a client-sent id.
3. Row-Level Security on every user table.
4. Isolate the data provider — only `app/src/lib/marketData/` may import Twelve Data/Finnhub.
5. It's a simulation — no real brokerage, no real money.

Full data model and logic in `ARCHITECTURE.md`.

## 5. The 8 frontend screens (what Lovable is building)

1. Landing page (marketing).
2. Auth (login + signup).
3. Dashboard — stat cards (portfolio value, buying power, today's change, total return), big value chart with range toggles, holdings table, watchlist + top movers sidebar.
4. Stock detail — price chart, key stats, buy/sell order panel (market + limit), tabs.
5. **What-If Simulator** — pick stock + past date + amount → value today, charted vs. S&P 500. The hero screen.
6. Markets — searchable/filterable stock list with sparklines.
7. Portfolio / Activity — allocation donut + transaction history.
8. Settings — profile, theme toggle, reset paper account to $100k.

Plus global shell: left sidebar nav, top bar with global search + avatar. Dark mode default. Every user starts with $100,000 virtual cash.

> Note: Lovable often "soft-builds" later screens — nails the first few, stubs the rest. Expect 1–3 follow-up prompts to flesh out thin screens (especially the Simulator). This is normal.
